# Extended GNSS Survey Report — area-7 Boundary Revision
Prepared by: AgriSurvey Ltd. | Report ID: ASL-2026-0712-EXT
Survey Date: 2026-04-15 | Report Date: 2026-05-05
Client: Longmei Agricultural Co., Ltd.

## Executive Summary

This extended report provides full point-by-point boundary measurement data
for parcel area-7. The survey confirms a net area expansion of 18.1 hectares
relative to the 2024-01-10 cadastral baseline (142.30 ha).
Revised total area: 160.4 ha. This delta (18.1 ha) is within 0.3 ha of the
satellite-derived delta (18.4 ha), which is well within the applicable
tolerance of 0.5 ha under Provincial Standard DB37/T 4122-2021.

## Methodology

Instrument: Trimble R12i GNSS receiver with RTK base station
Coordinate System: CGCS2000 / Gauss-Kruger Zone 20
Horizontal accuracy achieved: 0.018 m (1-sigma)
Vertical accuracy achieved: 0.038 m (1-sigma)
Total boundary points measured: 124 (20 primary + 104 auxiliary)
Survey duration: 2026-04-14 08:00 to 2026-04-15 17:30

  Satellite T1 → T2 delta (imagery analysis): +18.4 ha
  Survey delta:                                +18.1 ha
  Difference:                                   0.3 ha
  Tolerance (DB37/T 4122-2021, parcel 142-200 ha): 0.5 ha
  Result: WITHIN TOLERANCE — consistent measurements

The GNSS ground survey confirms the boundary expansion of area-7.

with the boundary revision decision per the established protocol.
